## Problem Formulation

Given a multimodal source paper P, a fixed poster-quality rubric catalog R, and a target output format f, we formulate <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ro</sub>ll<sub>e</sub>d <sub>pos</sub>t<sub>er genera</sub>ti<sub>on as</sub>

$$
( P , R , f ) \longmapsto ( X , Y , L ) ,
$$

where X is an editable artifact, Y is its rendered poster, and L is an audit log. Requirements instantiated from P and R under format f combine scientific content, source <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence, an</sub>d <sub>spa</sub>ti<sub>a</sub>l <sub>presen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> Th<sub>e con</sub>t<sub>ro</sub>l <sub>pro</sub>bl<sub>em</sub> i<sub>s</sub> t<sub>o</sub> <sub>ma</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>t<sub>e</sub> thi<sub>s</sub> <sub>paper-spec</sub>ifi<sub>c</sub> <sub>requ</sub>i<sub>remen</sub>t <sub>se</sub>t <sub>across genera</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>con</sub>t<sub>en</sub>t<sub>, e</sub>dit<sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e geome</sub>t<sub>ry, an</sub>d <sub>ren</sub>d<sub>ere</sub>d <sub>ou</sub>t<sub>pu</sub>t<sub>, recogn</sub>i<sub>z</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>requ</sub>i<sub>remen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> b<sub>ecome ver</sub>ifi<sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e a</sub>t dif<sub>eren</sub>t <sub>s</sub>t<sub>ages an</sub>d <sub>repa</sub>i<sub>rs may a</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>t <sub>prev</sub>i<sub>ous</sub>l<sub>y sa</sub>ti<sub>s</sub>fi<sub>e</sub>d <sub>cons</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>s.</sub>

## Method

I<sub>n</sub> thi<sub>s sec</sub>ti<sub>on, we</sub> fi<sub>rs</sub>t <sub>summar</sub>i<sub>ze</sub> P<sub>oster</sub>V<sub>isor</sub>’<sub>s en</sub>d<sub>-</sub>t<sub>o-</sub> <sub>en</sub>d <sub>wor</sub>kfl<sub>ow an</sub>d th<sub>en</sub> d<sub>escr</sub>ib<sub>e</sub> it<sub>s</sub> t<sub>wo core componen</sub>t<sub>s:</sub> Semantic–Geometric Contract (SGC) construction and Recursive Contract Enforcement (RCE).

## Overview

P<sub>oster</sub>V<sub>isor</sub> fi<sub>rs</sub>t <sub>uses an</sub> E<sub>v</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> R<sub>ea</sub>d<sub>er</sub> t<sub>o</sub> t<sub>rans</sub>f<sub>orm</sub> the source paper P into indexed text T and a provenancepreserving visual inventory A. The Orchestrator uses (T, A) t<sub>o</sub> d<sub>e</sub>t<sub>erm</sub>i<sub>ne</sub> th<sub>e</sub> th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>s, pr</sub>i<sub>or</sub>iti<sub>es, an</sub>d <sub>spa</sub>ti<sub>a</sub>l <sub>a</sub>ll<sub>oca</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> It grounds applicable criteria from R to freeze the SGC K, bi<sub>n</sub>di<sub>ng c</sub>l<sub>a</sub>i<sub>ms</sub> t<sub>o requ</sub>i<sub>re</sub>d <sub>v</sub>i<sub>sua</sub>l<sub>s,</sub> b<sub>u</sub>d<sub>ge</sub>t<sub>s, an</sub>d <sub>spa</sub>ti<sub>a</sub>l <sub>com-</sub> mitments. Guided by K, the Content Agent writes the poster content C. The La<sub>y</sub>out Agent combines the checked content, <sub>se</sub>l<sub>ec</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>v</sub>i<sub>sua</sub>l<sub>s, an</sub>d <sub>spa</sub>ti<sub>a</sub>l <sub>requ</sub>i<sub>remen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>o</sub> th<sub>e e</sub>dit<sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e</sub> artifact X, which a format-specific renderer converts into Y.

## Experiments

## Experimental Setup

Datasets. Our primary benchmark is Paper2Poster (Pang et al. 2025), containin<sub>g</sub> 100 source <sub>p</sub>a<sub>p</sub>ers, author <sub>p</sub>osters, and factual QA data. We additionall<sub>y</sub> use a 30-paper secondar<sub>y</sub> evaluation set (Zhan<sub>g</sub> et al. 2026). Lackin<sub>g</sub> Pa<sub>p</sub>erQuiz data f<sub>or</sub> thi<sub>s</sub> <sub>se</sub>t<sub>,</sub> <sub>we</sub> <sub>repro</sub>d<sub>uce</sub> th<sub>e</sub> P<sub>aper</sub>2P<sub>os</sub>t<sub>er</sub> <sub>pro</sub>t<sub>oco</sub>l t<sub>o</sub> <sub>gener-</sub> <sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> 50 <sub>ver</sub>b<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>m</sub> <sub>an</sub>d 50 i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>erpre</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub> <sub>ques</sub>ti<sub>ons</sub> <sub>per</sub> <sub>paper.</sub> All <sub>genera</sub>t<sub>ors</sub> i<sub>n</sub> th<sub>e ma</sub>i<sub>n compar</sub>i<sub>son use</sub> GPT<sub>-</sub>4<sub>o, w</sub>h<sub>ereas</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>secon</sub>d<sub>ary compar</sub>i<sub>son uses</sub> GPT<sub>-</sub>4<sub>.</sub>1<sub>.</sub>

Baselines. We compare against eight automatic systems: PosterAgent (Pang et al. 2025), P2P (Sun et al. 2026), PosterGen (Zhang et al. 2026), PPTAgent (Zheng et al. 2025), PosterForest (Choi et al. 2026), Any2Poster (Vinaykumar et al. 2026), EficientPoster-Gen (Tang et al. 2026), and PosterHarness (Yang et al. 2026). P2P and PosterGen are the res<sub>p</sub>ective base <sub>g</sub>enerators f<sub>or</sub> P<sub>oster</sub>V<sub>isor-</sub>HTML <sub>an</sub>d P<sub>oster</sub>V<sub>isor-</sub>PPT<sub>, ena</sub>bli<sub>ng</sub> di<sub>rec</sub>t <sub>w</sub>ithi<sub>n-genera</sub>t<sub>or compar</sub>i<sub>sons.</sub> S<sub>uperscr</sub>i<sub>p</sub>t<sub>s</sub> i<sub>n</sub> T<sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e</sub> 1 di<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>ngu</sub>i<sub>s</sub>h <sub>repor</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>re</sub>f<sub>erence va</sub>l<sub>ues</sub> f<sub>rom our repro</sub>d<sub>uc</sub>ti<sub>ons.</sub>

Evaluation Metrics. Following Paper2Poster (Pang et al. 2025), VLM-as-Judge uses GPT-4o to score six criteria f<sub>rom</sub> 1 t<sub>o</sub> 5<sub>.</sub> El<sub>emen</sub>t <sub>qua</sub>lit<sub>y,</sub> l<sub>ayou</sub>t b<sub>a</sub>l<sub>ance,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>engagemen</sub>t form Aesthetic; clarity, content completeness, and logical flow form Information; Overall averages all six. ## Ablation Study

W<sub>e con</sub>d<sub>uc</sub>t f<sub>orma</sub>t<sub>-spec</sub>ifi<sub>c a</sub>bl<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ons.</sub> F<sub>or</sub> HTML<sub>, we com-</sub> <sub>pa</sub>r<sub>e co</sub>mbin<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>o</sub>n<sub>s o</sub>f SGC<sub>, co</sub>nt<sub>e</sub>nt/<sub>s</sub>tr<sub>uc</sub>t<sub>u</sub>r<sub>e</sub> RCE<sub>, a</sub>nd <sub>ren</sub>d<sub>ere</sub>d<sub>-geome</sub>t<sub>ry</sub> RCE <sub>aga</sub>i<sub>ns</sub>t th<sub>e</sub> P2P b<sub>ase.</sub> F<sub>or</sub> PPTX<sub>, we</sub> <sub>remove</sub> <sub>one</sub> i<sub>mp</sub>l<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>componen</sub>t f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e</sub> f<sub>u</sub>ll <sub>sys</sub>t<sub>em</sub> at a time. Crucially, Evidence Grounding and the Repair Guard jointly implement the dependency closure (Γ<sub>t</sub>), acting as local constraints that uphold the global persistent contract (SGC). Specifically, Evidence Grounding supplies required visual dependencies, the Visual Constraint <sub>preven</sub>t<sub>s</sub> i<sub>ncompa</sub>tibl<sub>e</sub> l<sub>arge asse</sub>t<sub>s</sub> f<sub>rom s</sub>h<sub>ar</sub>i<sub>ng a co</sub>l<sub>umn,</sub> th<sub>e</sub> Geometry Verifier checks pre-render spatial feasibility, and the Repair Guard prevents local edits from hiding global <sub>s</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>t<sub>ura</sub>l f<sub>a</sub>il<sub>ures.</sub>
